 THIS PETITION, COMING ON FOR ORDERS, THIS DAY, THE
COURT MADE THE FOLLOWING:

 - 311 -
NC: 2024:KHC-D:280
WP No. 107797 of 2023
ORDER
This petition is filed calling in question the ineligible
voters list published for the election to the Board of Directors of
the respondent No.5, which was scheduled to be held on
23.12.2023. This Court vide order dated 20.12.2023 permitted
the petitioners to vote in the election to be held on 23.12.2023,
however, directed the Returning Officer not to announce the
results of the election. In light of the interim order granted by
this Court, the petitioners have casted their votes in the
election, which was held on 23.12.2023. In identical
circumstances, the coordinate Bench of this Court in WP
No.8502/2022 a/w WP No.8477/2022 disposed of on 7.6.2022
called upon the Returning Officer to announce the result
forthwith including the votes cast by the petitioners therein.
Therefore, this petition also requires to be disposed of in light
of the order passed by this Court in WP No.8502/2022 and
connected matter to maintain parity. Accordingly, I pass the
following:
ORDER
i) The petition is allowed.
ii) The respondent No.4 is directed to announce the
result forthwith including the votes cast by the
petitioners.
Sd/-
 JUDGE
